The exact forms support cancellation and comparison without
introducing a display tolerance into the underlying identity. A
decimal representation is useful for communicating scale, but
it should not replace the rational source when the claim concerns exact equality.
The normalized-square asymmetry relation is an example.
Its coefﬁcient follows from the exact difference between two
source channels. Storing a rounded decimal for each channel
and subtracting them can introduce a residual that belongs to
the representation rather than the model. Retaining the rational
forms makes the source of exactness explicit and separates it
from the precision used for later measured comparisons.
Exact representation does not remove the need for units. The
rational 7117/768 is a structural coefﬁcient in the declared
channel. A measurement in MeV or atomic mass units enters
through its own conversion and comparison speciﬁcation. Exactness of the rational coefﬁcient does not turn that conversion
into a derived physical law.
Undeﬁned expressions require equal care. The K = X = 0
carrier row remains part of the lift table while Y = X/K is
undeﬁned. A receiving program that ﬁlls the quotient with
zero would preserve the table’s length but change its mathematical content. Filling it with one would impose a different
unsupported convention. The appropriate representation distinguishes a retained row with an inapplicable or undeﬁned quotient from an ordinary numerical value.
This distinction also affects aggregate statistics. A reported
closure of 138 evaluable quotients is not a failure to account
for a 139-row table. It is a statement that one row lies outside
the quotient’s domain. A computation that silently includes an
invented endpoint could appear more complete while actually
weakening the speciﬁcation.

XIX.2 The row as a serializable scientiﬁc object
The row identity introduced in Chapter I contains a source table, schema, address and acting operator. A computational representation must retain enough of that identity to distinguish
rows whose numerical ﬁelds happen to agree. It must also retain the difference between an input and a derived ﬁeld.
For example, a native value, a debit and a resulting readout
are three related ﬁelds. If the representation stores only the
readout, a null result could be indistinguishable from a row that
never had a native candidate. The LC06 witness shows why the
history matters: native value 18 and debit 18 produce a null
readout, and that cancellation has a different meaning from an
absent record. Exact arithmetic can preserve the cancellation
only if the operands and their types remain available.
Likewise, a known particle or element label should remain a
reveal ﬁeld. Moving the label into the address-generation stage
would alter the inference order even if the displayed table remained unchanged. The computational interface must preserve
which information was available at construction, which was
used for selection and which was opened only for comparison.
The same applies to admission. A nonzero native value is
88

<!-- page 89 -->

SAM Volume II | Chapter XIX

Brady | 19 October 2026

information that the aggregate omits. The geometry is reconstructed from that information under a declared procedure.
The subsequent delivery test shows that a retained symmetry
can survive some losses even though the exact shape changes.
A successful serialization must therefore preserve more than
the diagnostic that happens to remain constant.
The relation between an informational receipt and a physical event remains a separate interpretive question. An exact
reversible record can demonstrate ﬁdelity within a ﬁnite computational model. It does not automatically establish a physical energy, a measured length or a causal matter-generation
process. Keeping the receipt exact is nevertheless valuable: it
preserves the construction so that its actual content can be inspected without relying on a picture or a compressed summary.

XIX.4 Source inventories and executable grammars
The executable N100 basis contains one hundred typed computation rows. It is not a replacement census for the physicalsource surfaces. The matter volume has already used 35 compact mass rows, 139 lift-table rows, 321 catalog rows, 126 inventory rows, an 80-row stable structural surface and a 126row periodic surface. Their counts remain attached to their
own selection predicates.
A computation may prune or select a basis for an operation.
The cross-volume source gives the example of removing ﬁve
p:9 rows from an executable basis. Such a selection does not
delete rows from the particle catalog, the mass table or the periodic source. It changes the operation’s admitted domain. The
source inventories must remain recoverable without applying
the computation’s pruning as a scientiﬁc revision.
This is analogous to the distinction between a table and a
query over the table, but the consequences here are mathematical. An executable basis can omit a source row because the
chosen operator is not deﬁned on it, because it is redundant
for a particular representation, or because the operation uses
a narrower class. None of those possibilities is equivalent to
declaring that the source row is physically nonexistent or experimentally rejected.
A program can therefore consume a 126-row periodic input
while using a 100-row operation grammar. It can compare a
139-row lift table while evaluating only 138 quotients. It can
preserve a 321-row catalog that includes carriers and rejected
candidates while selecting a smaller matter-allowed subset for
source support. Correctness requires the selection rule and its
domain, not equality of all counts.
The source-authorized N100-to-F81 elementwise join remains absent in the inspected record. Its aggregate contact is
not enough to ﬁll the missing assignment. A receiving computation can carry both source and target objects and can record
that the join is undeﬁned; it need not invent the map to remain
a complete representation of the current state.

XIX.6 Empirical ﬁelds retain their provenance
The matter account includes several kinds of empirical contact. A neutrino absolute scale uses a measured splitting. A
historical nuclear readout uses ﬁtted coefﬁcients and then evaluates a frozen extension. B5T identiﬁes an informational tensor before binding targets are opened, then evaluates a binding
residual lane with its own development and held data. A targetvisible He-4 discovery calculation uses information that a blind
conﬁrmation would withhold.
These histories remain part of the exported object. A numerical prediction cannot be interpreted solely from its ﬁnal
residual. The receiving program or report must preserve which
quantities were ﬁxed, which were ﬁt, which targets were visible, which rows were held and which controls were part of
the declared test. The same residual can mean different things
under different information histories.
A useful example is the phrase “zero reﬁt.” For CR277
it identiﬁes the extension stage: the earlier ﬁtted operator is
frozen and applied to a broader representative table. It does
not say that the original coefﬁcients were never ﬁt. Similarly,
a typed-rational coefﬁcient selected near a ﬁtted value retains
that selection history even when the ﬁnal displayed coefﬁcient
is exact. Exact arithmetic and empirical selection are compatible, but their combination is not the same as a coefﬁcient derived without data contact.
The B5T target-blind tensor stage also must be stated precisely. Freezing the assignment before binding is opened prevents binding from selecting that assignment or breaking its
tie. It does not mean that every subsequent component of the
binding prediction is unsupervised or that the measured scale
is derived from a ﬁnite permutation alone. The representation
and the residual test have related but distinct evidence burdens.
An executable record is most useful when it preserves these
distinctions rather than replacing them with a single PASS ﬂag.
The ﬂag identiﬁes the outcome of declared predicates. The
predicates, inputs, controls and classiﬁcation explain what that
outcome means.

XIX.5 Writes receipts and retained history
The W8/X1/W9 lifecycle supplies a computation-facing language for a supported relation, activation and completion. In
the current typed reading, these roles are distinct from homogeneous mass-lift tensors. Theta18 supplies a carrier or
completion-report role at the relevant interface, while the Aﬁeld carrier supplies transport. The matter volume exports
these meanings rather than merely the displayed values 8, 1,
9 and 18.
The reception results show why an endpoint value can be
an insufﬁcient record. Different relation histories or incomplete delivery patterns can preserve an aggregate number while
changing the reconstructed object. A computation that retains
only the ﬁnal count would lose the information needed to distinguish those cases. Exact receipts preserve relation identity,
direction, address and payload at the scope established by the
source test.
The AFC2 and AFC3 chain is a concrete example. A scalar
coordinate such as u = d = 9 does not identify the six-object
incidence structure. The eighteen target-barred records carry

binding account did not supply the nonlinear binding readout. The two CR283 geometry searches returned no valid
maps. Frozen hydrogen-ladder energy transfers missed their
measured targets. Target-visible reﬁnements remain discoveries rather than conﬁrmations.
A computational export that retains only successful rows
would make the source record less informative. The failures
distinguish quantities that would otherwise be easy to merge.
The direct mass failure separates coupling from rest-source
information. The failed geometry searches separate cardinality from placement. The energy-transfer misses separate exact
structural graphs from a calibrated physical law.
Failures also need their scope. A failed speciﬁc map has the
scope of the tested mapping. A failed frozen energy expression does not invalidate every relation in the graph on which it
was evaluated. Conversely, a surviving graph or exact identity
does not rescue the rejected physical prediction. The export
must retain the proposition tested, not merely a favorable or
unfavorable label.
This principle avoids two opposite distortions. One would
erase useful exact work whenever a stronger interpretation
fails. The other would use useful exact work to imply that
the stronger interpretation succeeded. The ﬁnite record supports neither shortcut. It permits each result to retain the status
earned by its own evidence.

mere list of claims. The exact algebra can be followed directly,
the deﬁnitions can be stated without ambiguity, and the historical tests can be described with their controls and limitations.
The resulting account is complete at the level it declares: an organized mathematical construction and its recorded evidence,
with physical identiﬁcations bounded by the evidence actually
available.
XIX.9 The matter account at its stopping point
The dedicated spin section adds exact joint state counting, controlled thermal ordering, microscopic memory, composition
cancellation, source-speciﬁc kinetic clocks and the latest paid
physical response to the matter construction. These pass to
Volume III as source-bound exact factors, generators, certiﬁed
matrix operands and retained dependency closures.
The work developed here has a deﬁnite ﬁnite content. It
supplies a typed distinction between substrate support and admitted matter; complementary carrier and retained channels;
several explicitly different particle and matter surfaces; classdependent generator and lift laws; conditional Higgs and neutrino packets; a four-slot nuclear ledger; an exact channelgap asymmetry relation; historical ﬁtted and later held binding readouts; periodic-family and isotope organization; ﬁnite
reception and reconstruction results; complete conditional nuclear and thermal sources; causal ordered contact histories; occupied Pauli atoms; full-motion molecular recipients; isotope
recoil transfer; a held light-source test; and an explicit common
physical energy convention.
It also has deﬁnite limits. Candidate uniqueness does not
follow from a small residual. The absolute neutrino scale retains its external anchor. A periodic frontier identity is not
an observation. The completed atomic and molecular recipients retain their declared source and prepared-family scopes.
An informational tensor or unscaled graph embedding is not
yet a calibrated physical nuclear law. The later chapters identify the achieved source, ﬁeld, occupied-state and outgoingrecord maps, together with the speciﬁc physical assignments
still open. These statements are the boundary of the present
account, not a schedule of obligations appended to it.
Volume III receives the objects on the established side of
that boundary together with the statuses that protect their meaning. It can preserve them, transform them under declared operators and examine their computational consequences. It need
not solve every open physical question before doing so. The
ﬁnite handoff is thus the appropriate end of the matter volume:
exact structure where the construction is exact, recorded comparison where the evidence is empirical, and explicit limits
where the source has not completed the bridge.

XIX.8 Source custody for the completed account
The continuous matter synthesis in SAMA supplies the historical narrative foundations. The expanded chapters receive the
later completed source reports, derivations and current authority summarized in the source register. Its frozen repository
version preserves the source chapters and the copied Courtroom material. The SAM Research Project technical spine
supplies the source-bound recovery map, while its live binding
and ATOM3D documents control the current status of those
branches. The evidence-rich unapproved review assembly supplies focused derivation and deviation chains without conferring author approval by its existence.[1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
The sources have different authority roles. A later prose assembly does not automatically supersede an executed result. A
repository reorganization does not change a scientiﬁc classiﬁcation. A historical result remains evidence for its own test
even when a later domain authority supplies the current synthesis. Where a source identiﬁes a missing permanent test key,
the missing key remains a custody boundary rather than being
replaced by a similar identiﬁer.
The numerical receipts quoted in this book are reported from
those records unless the text explicitly describes code inspection. The preparation of this manuscript did not rerun scientiﬁc campaigns, reﬁt coefﬁcients or reproduce every independent validator. Selected code inspection establishes what the
inspected routine does, such as retaining a ﬁtted coefﬁcient
when a typed substitution falls outside its threshold. It does
not substitute for execution of every dependency or independent replication of the entire research program.
This distinction does not reduce a source-based book to a

# Run results received into Volume II (October 2026, engineering register)

An addition to the living text, not part of the submitted manuscript above: what the GEN6 Duck runs of 20–22 October 2026 showed that bears on Volume II, by chapter,
with the campaign and history entry that carry each statement. Labels as in Volume I's addition: **result** (natively certified receipt; engineering labels only),
**reading**, **idea** (open; only Sean Brady changes a status). Units kT = 1, nats. The four-state prototype is the declared two-spin analogue (energies 0, 1, 2, 0; β = 1;
attempts 1/2; visible bit 0, hidden bit 1; hidden spin s = (−1, −1, 1, 1)); records are two readings at separation 1; a staircase is one admitted library recipe
(standing requirement, Sean Brady 2026-10-21). Directed by Sean Brady. Executed with Claude Code. Earlier credit (ChatGPT, Codex) is unchanged.

## Chapters VII–IX — spin memory, records and the staircase engine

- **Result.** The headline staircase values of the prototype are reproduced by three implementations with exhaustive per-chain optima (spin_verify_1, H002760; the
  verification form adopted 2026-10-21): the one-stage gain 0.0087532343 at dwell 4 (0.387 of the record's information ceiling I = 0.0226357492536), the two-stage gain
  0.0119173272 on the 1/20 family (0.527), the four-stage gain 0.0159283 at total time 16 (0.704), and the seven-stage share 0.765 above the four-stage stationary 0.756.
  The grid artefact of the earlier runs is understood: a gain is a difference of two separately maximised works, so a coarse family inflates it and can reverse
  orderings; the comparison family is each policy's optimum on one fine family, and orderings below the family spread are unresolved (spin_work_4 H002… series,
  spin_verify_1).
- **Result.** The flag record keeps a net-price advantage through a staircase (gain per net price 0.0400 against 0.0319 clean at two stages, +25 % relative, witnessed;
  spin_work_4); no cycle closes; the best working net-price share is about 4 %, half of I/B.
- **Result.** The Chapter IX sub-source (one port plus hidden spins, declared) converts the same share of its own ceiling as the prototype (volii_matter_1, H002762); the
  record's information ceiling has a native route (one exact product forming the marginals of the four branch records, four log traces, one combine), and share
  orderings are witnessed by cross-multiplication without division.

## Chapter XV — common action, the two recipients of a lapse, the switching ledger

- **Result.** A lapse N has two recipients in the matter engine, separated natively (volii_matter_1, H002762). Recipient A (clock N on every state) is a time rescaling:
  the dwell-d map at lapse N equals the unlapsed dwell-N·d map, invisible in ticks, and the two lapse orders of the ordered clock give the same gain to every digit
  (identity recipe, 62 checks, control failed as declared; volii_matter_2, H002763). Recipient B (energies N·E₀, field unscaled, clock 1) changes the Gibbs law the
  record reads: the ceiling and gain fall, the share of the ceiling rises as N falls (0.527, 0.557, 0.579, 0.599 at N = 1, 3/4, 1/2, 1/4, every adjacent ordering witnessed
  by disjoint cross products; volii_matter_3, H002764), and when the lapse changes between stages a switching term (N_k − N_{k+1})·E₀·q_k is paid, native since library
  revision 2.
- **Result.** Under recipient B the quantity to optimise is the field-plus-lapse total. Every chain at its total optimum, the first lapse order (4/5 then 3/5) keeps a
  total lead of 1.83e-4 to 1.90e-4 over the reverse on five field families, each witnessed with its negation failing as declared; the totals agree across families to
  under 1e-5 while the lapse term does not (it flips sign between families) and which ledger carries the ordering reverses (volii_matter_3 H002764, volii_matter_4
  H002765). The entry switch contributes exactly zero on nine receipts; the interior and exit switches both move between families; on nested grids the family move is
  carried wholly by one policy.
- **Result.** At each chain's continuous optimum (host Newton, rounded to 1/4096) the total is a certified local maximum against four axis neighbours on all twelve chains
  of both orders, while the field work alone and the lapse work alone each fail the same cross: their gradients are equal and opposite at the total's optimum, which is
  the mechanism of the family dependence (volii_matter_5, H002766). The lead's earlier mechanism (a soft Hessian direction) is refuted and recorded.
- **Result.** Under four fixed conventions set by Sean Brady (records prepared on the unlapsed source; the lapse list (1, N, N, 1) paid by the native ledger; the
  denominator I(1) for every N; both policies at their total optimum) the switched share at N = 1/4 is above N = 1/2 on three families (gaps of the total +3.05e-5,
  +2.16e-5, +2.42e-5, witnessed; spread 8.9e-6, which is also the gap's own drift, so the ordering is resolved by a factor of two to three); the switched curve on the
  second family rises with diminishing increments; the exit-switch identity gain_lapse = −(1 − N)(Σ_h − Σ_c)·E₀·q₂ holds on every family with controls (volii_matter_6,
  H002767). The mixed recipient (clock N^a, energies N^(1−a)) at a = 1/2 folds into recipient B at map level (identity passed, control failed as declared).
- **Result, cross-volume.** Recipient A's order-blindness is Volume I's inertia identity inside this chapter's engine (see Volume I's addition).
- **Idea (open).** The Volume I per-event switching work W_i = E_{i+1}(x_i) − E_i(x_i) is the same ledger as recipient B's lapse term; a coupled binding driven by a
  per-event lapse would connect the volumes properly and waits on the three definition choices.

## Chapters V and XV — binding constructions

- **Result.** The `coupled` quadratic-action binding construction (source metric, inverse inertia, coupling g, canonical state, reservoir) ran for the first time on the
  18-coordinate Volume II spin carrier (41 segments) and its switching ledger carries the prototype's identities (volii_matter_1, H002762).

## Instruments built for this volume (recipe library, ironclad)

The staircase library (one admitted recipe per staircase; revision 2 returns every stage state and the lapse ledger; revision 3 adds probe chains and per-chain works),
the staircase front (generators, maps, records and the ceiling in one method; a case to its witness in seven tasks; cross-checked by the classic route to 1e-40), and
the identities library. Program notes: a method admits at most 64 steps; an admitted method is visible only to the controller that admitted it; about 45 tasks per
peer per 1500 s window with chain steps ahead of new maps.
